# Phase-7 Wave 4 industry-specific repository joins — P7-INDUSTRY-JOINS-W4

## Scope and evidence boundary

This is a bounded page-3 public-metadata discovery tranche in a new `wave-4/` subdirectory. It expands the five Luna discovery buckets for all 17 industries, excludes every Wave-1, Wave-2, and Wave-3 canonical identity, and emits at most 20 unique page-3 join attempts per bucket. It is not a complete-pair or overall Phase-7 completion claim.

- Industries: 17; discovery buckets: 85; per-bucket ceiling: 20; target positions: 1,700.
- Wave-4 page-3 join records: 931; selected metadata-level joins: 602; held/unknown: 317; rejected: 12.
- Underfilled buckets: 52/85; quota-reached buckets: 33; no bucket was padded or forced.
- Unique canonical identities and records: 931 / 931; Wave-1/Wave-2/Wave-3 identity overlap: 0; Wave-4 duplicate records: 0.
- Discovery: 85 unauthenticated GitHub Search API page-3 queries, 93 HTTP attempts, 8 rate-limit reset events preserved; repository pages/README/source were not fetched.
- Every row is reference-only, with rights uncleared, SBOM unknown/not scanned, and execution `UNEXECUTED` / admission `NOT_ADMITTED`.

## Quality, rights, and no-admission ledger

- Source class is first-party unauthenticated GitHub Search API page-3 repository metadata only. Search result descriptions and repository metadata are discovery evidence, not README, source, capability, adoption, or implementation proof.
- Query, page, observed date, result count, HTTP status, access state, and rate-limit headers/events are preserved per bucket and per row. Eight rate-limit reset events were encountered and retained.
- License signals are SPDX where returned or NOASSERTION; rights are not cleared. Sponsorship, contributor provenance, notices, retention, SBOM, runtime, cost, and support remain unknown unless explicitly stated as metadata-only.
- Generic CRUD, starter, scaffold, template, clone, and thin dashboard matches stay hold; archived/fork metadata stays reject. Underfilled buckets remain gaps.
- Consequential industries remain synthetic/read-only administrative wedges: no clinical, legal, underwriting, mortgage, hiring, payment, booking, dispatch, publishing, or external side effect is claimed.

## Post-write smoke contract

- Recompute exact JSONL row counts, unique artifact IDs, unique industry×canonical repository identities, 17-industry and 85-bucket coverage, Wave-1/Wave-2/Wave-3 overlap, and row-derived status totals.
- Recompute page-3 query/date/access/rate-limit provenance, selected/hold/reject semantics, rights/SBOM/maintenance unknowns, falsifiers, and all no-action boundary fields.
- Verify Markdown table counts, local links, no executable code fence, and git diff --check.
- Fresh CENA callback delivery verified in herdr-2 pane w659e02f80e5bb1-1 after a 2-second readback and Enter-only retry; this is not an overall Phase-7 completion claim.

## Lane boundary

Research-only; execution `UNEXECUTED`; admission `NOT_ADMITTED`; implementation false; shared Phase-7 state unpromoted; parent goal active. No overall Phase-7 completion is claimed.
